tool_result: 140	def _minute_specs(today_iso):
141	    """≤60-day minute slices 2026-07-15 → today, per instrument (Kite's minute range cap)."""
142	    from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
143	    start = datetime.fromisoformat(SINCE).date()
144	    end = datetime.fromisoformat(today_iso).date()
145	    chunks = []
146	    a = start
147	    while a <= end:
148	        b = min(a + timedelta(days=55), end)
149	        chunks.append((a.isoformat(), b.isoformat()))
150	        a = b + timedelta(days=1)
151	    specs = []
152	    for tok in ds.frozen_tokens():
153	        for frm, to in chunks:
154	            specs.append((str(tok), "minute", frm, to))
155	    return specs

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime
import os
import subprocess

REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))


def _ist_now():
    return datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone(datetime.timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))


def in_safety_window(now=None):
    """True inside 05:30–15:45 IST — the no-restart window (SAFETY ruling)."""
    now = now or _ist_now()
    hm = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
    return 5 * 60 + 30 <= hm <= 15 * 60 + 45


def _head_commit():
    try:
        return subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"], cwd=REPO,
                              capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
    except Exception:
        return "?"

47	        last = led.drop_duplicates("trade_id", keep="last")
48	        for _, r in last[last["status"] == "queued"].iterrows():
49	            queued.append({"stock": r.get("stock"), "side": r.get("direction"), "sentence": r.get("pattern")})
50	    line = TC.preopen(queued)
51	    notify(line)
52	    _append_feed(line, "preopen")
53	    return line

55	
56	def summary():
57	    led = paper.read_ledger()
58	    today = datetime.date.today().isoformat()
59	    trades = wins = losses = 0
60	    net = inverse = 0.0
61	    by_engine = {}
62	    if len(led):
63	        last = led.drop_duplicates("trade_id", keep="last")
64	        closed = last[(last["status"] == "closed") &
65	                      last["exit_date"].astype(str).str.startswith(today)].copy()
66	        closed["r"] = pd.to_numeric(closed.get("result_rupees"), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
67	        trades = int(len(closed))
68	        wins = int((closed["r"] > 0).sum()); losses = int((closed["r"] < 0).sum())
69	        net = round(float(closed["r"].sum()), 0)
70	        inverse = round(-net, 0)                          # every call flipped = the mirror P&L
71	        by_engine = {"E1": round(float(closed["r"].sum()), 0)} if trades else {}
72	    ctx = TC.ledger_context()
73	    all_closed = 0
74	    all_win = 0.0
75	    if len(led):
76	        lastc = led.drop_duplicates("trade_id", keep="last")
77	        cl = lastc[lastc["status"] == "closed"]
78	        r = pd.to_numeric(cl.get("result_rupees"), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
79	        all_closed = int(len(r)); all_win = round(float((r > 0).mean() * 100), 0) if all_closed else 0
80	    s = {"date": today, "trades": trades, "wins": wins, "losses": losses, "net_rupees": net,
81	         "inverse_rupees": inverse, "by_engine": by_engine, "regime": _weather(),
82	         "all_trades": all_closed, "all_win_pct": all_win, "all_pnl_rupees": ctx["pnl_all"],
83	         "capital": paper.NOTIONAL_CAPITAL}
84	    line = TC.day_summary(s)
85	    notify(line)
86	    _append_feed(line, "summary")
87	    try:                                                  # the digest carries the day summary
88	        json.dump(s, open(os.path.join(ds._REPO, "reports", "E1-DAY-SUMMARY.json"), "w"), indent=1)
89	    except Exception:
90	        pass
91	    return line

18	def line(status, expected_cameras=1):
19	    """The 08:15 line for `status`. `status`: {streams:{name:{fresh:bool}}, orders_allowed_now, gates:{hold_reason},
20	    queue_loaded:bool, orders_queued:int}. Every gate must pass for READY."""
21	    streams = status.get("streams") or {}
22	    up = [n for n, v in streams.items() if (v or {}).get("fresh")]
23	    down = [n for n, v in streams.items() if not (v or {}).get("fresh")]
24	    gate_open = bool(status.get("orders_allowed_now"))
25	    queue_loaded = bool(status.get("queue_loaded"))
26	    n_queued = int(status.get("orders_queued") or 0)
27	    problems = []
28	    if len(up) < expected_cameras:
29	        problems.append("cameras down: %s" % (", ".join(down) if down else "none reporting"))
30	    if not gate_open:
31	        reason = (status.get("gates") or {}).get("hold_reason") or "not open"
32	        problems.append("order gate HELD (%s)" % reason)
33	    if not queue_loaded:
34	        problems.append("tomorrow's queue not loaded")
35	    if problems:
36	        return "🙋 NOT READY — %s — %s" % ("; ".join(problems), CLOSER)
37	    return "✅ READY — %d cameras up, gate open, %d orders queued" % (len(up), n_queued)

tool_result: 1	"""R-BUILD-JOURNAL + R-BUILD-TRANSCRIPT + R-EYES — the public /build/ room, rebuilt every light cycle.
2	
3	Four things under site/build/, all scrubbed of secrets FIRST (never written unscrubbed):
4	  - index.html   : links to status, journal, transcript and commits.
5	  - journal.html : append-only, machine-written — ruling in progress, section/item, last commit hash+time,
6	                   tests passed/failed, files touched, next item, and the last self-flag line.
7	  - <slug>.txt   : this build session's own transcript (tool calls, outputs, messages), plain text, at an
8	                   unguessable filename; scrubbed.
9	  - commits.html : every commit tonight — hash, time, message, files touched, full diff, newest first; scrubbed.
10	
11	THE SCRUBBER IS THE SAFETY WALL. It replaces every API key, token, password, Telegram id, cookie and absolute
12	user path with [redacted] — exact secret values loaded from their stores at runtime PLUS shape patterns. It is
13	fail-closed: if scrubbing a file raises, that file is NOT published. A test (tests/test_build_scrub.py) plants
14	the known secret shapes and proves they are caught. No keys, no tokens, no owner names ever reach site/build/.
15	"""
16	from __future__ import annotations
17	
18	import glob
19	import html as _html
20	import json
21	import os
22	import re
23	import subprocess
24	import sys
25	from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
26	
27	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
28	from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
29	
30	REPO = ds._REPO
31	IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
32	OUT = os.path.join(REPO, "site", "build")
33	JOURNAL_LOG = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-JOURNAL.log")   # append-only
34	STATE_FILE = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "BUILD-STATE.json")     # header fields the session sets
35	SLUG_FILE = os.path.join(REPO, "scheduler", ".build_slug")        # stable unguessable transcript name (gitignored)
36	REDACT = "[redacted]"
37	
38	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
39	# THE SCRUBBER
40	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
41	_OWNER_WORDS = ["[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]", "[redacted]"]
42	
43	# keyed assignments: keep the key, redact the value (access_token: "abc" -> access_token: [redacted])
44	_KEYED = re.compile(
45	    r'(?i)\b(access[_-]?token|api[_-]?key|api[_-]?secret|client[_-]?secret|secret|password|passwd|pwd|'
46	    r'authorization|bearer|cookie|set-cookie|chat[_-]?id|bot[_-]?token|refresh[_-]?token|private[_-]?key|token)'
47	    r'(["\']?\s*[:=]\s*["\']?)([A-Za-z0-9._\-/+:]{4,})')
48	# run BEFORE _KEYED: these consume values that span spaces (Bearer <jwt>) or whole cookie values, which the
49	# key=value rule would only half-catch.
50	_PRE = [
51	    (re.compile(r'(?i)\bBearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._\-]{8,}'), REDACT),                 # Authorization: [redacted] <jwt>
52	    (re.compile(r'(?i)((?:set-)?cookie\s*[:=]\s*)[^\r\n]+'), r'\1' + REDACT),    # Cookie: [redacted]
53	]
54	_SHAPES = [
55	    re.compile(r'gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}'),               # GitHub PAT (classic)
56	    re.compile(r'github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}'),             # GitHub PAT (fine-grained)
57	    re.compile(r'\b\d{6,12}:[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}\b'),          # Telegram bot token (id:secret, any length)
58	    re.compile(r'\bxox[baprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}'),           # Slack
59	    re.compile(r'\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9._\-]{10,}'),                # a bare JWT (starts eyJ)
60	    re.compile(r'\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b'),  # any email address
61	]
62	# absolute user-home paths, both slash styles: [redacted] [redacted] [redacted] [redacted] [redacted]
63	_PATHS = re.compile(r'(?i)(?:[A-Z]:\\+Users\\+|[redacted][redacted][redacted]\\/\s"\']+')
64	
65	
66	def _secret_literals():
67	    """Exact secret VALUES pulled live from their stores (all gitignored), so real credentials are redacted
68	    verbatim wherever they appear. Never returns anything to a caller but the scrubber."""
69	    vals = set()
70	    try:
71	        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, ".kite_token.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
72	        for k in ("access_token", "api_key", "api_secret"):
73	            if d.get(k):
74	                vals.add(str(d[k]))
75	    except Exception:
76	        pass
77	    for k in ("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID", "GITHUB_TOKEN", "GH_TOKEN", "KITE_API_SECRET",
78	              "KITE_API_KEY", "HEALTHCHECKS_URL"):
79	        v = os.environ.get(k)
80	        if v and len(v) >= 5:
81	            vals.add(v)
82	    for bat in ("SET_GIT_TOKEN.bat", "TOKEN.bat"):
83	        try:
84	            txt = open(os.path.join(REPO, bat), encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read()
85	            vals |= set(re.findall(r'gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}|github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}', txt))
86	        except Exception:
87	            pass
88	    return {v for v in vals if v and len(str(v)) >= 5}
89	
90	
91	def scrub(text):
92	    """Redact every secret/PII shape. FAIL-CLOSED at the call site: a caller that cannot scrub does not write.
93	    Order: exact live secret values first, then keyed assignments, then shapes, then owner words, then paths."""
94	    if text is None:
95	        return ""
96	    t = str(text)
97	    for v in sorted(_secret_literals(), key=len, reverse=True):
98	        t = t.replace(v, REDACT)
99	    for pat, repl in _PRE:
100	        t = pat.sub(repl, t)
101	    for pat in _SHAPES:
102	        t = pat.sub(REDACT, t)
103	    t = _KEYED.sub(lambda m: m.group(1) + m.group(2) + REDACT, t)
104	    for w in _OWNER_WORDS:
105	        t = re.sub(re.escape(w), REDACT, t, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
106	    t = _PATHS.sub(REDACT, t)
107	    return t

113	ITEM_TIMES = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "ITEM-TIMES.json")   # {item: {start, finish}} — R-ETA measured durations

116	def set_state(**kw):
117	    """Update the journal header fields. Called by the build session as it works (machine-written from the
118	    session's own state, not typed prose). On an item change, stamps the old item's finish and the new one's
119	    start into ITEM-TIMES.json so R-ETA can measure real durations."""
120	    st = _state()
121	    old_item = st.get("item")
122	    st.update({k: v for k, v in kw.items() if v is not None})
123	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(STATE_FILE), exist_ok=True)
124	    with open(STATE_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
125	        json.dump(st, f, indent=1)
126	    new_item = st.get("item")
127	    if kw.get("item") and new_item != old_item:
128	        _stamp_item(old_item, new_item)
129	    return st
130	
131	
132	def _stamp_item(old_item, new_item):
133	    try:
134	        times = json.load(open(ITEM_TIMES, encoding="utf-8"))
135	    except Exception:
136	        times = {}
137	    now = _now().isoformat()
138	    if old_item and old_item in times and not times[old_item].get("finish"):
139	        times[old_item]["finish"] = now
140	    if new_item:
141	        times.setdefault(new_item, {"start": now, "finish": None})
142	    try:
143	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(ITEM_TIMES), exist_ok=True)
144	        json.dump(times, open(ITEM_TIMES, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)
145	    except Exception:
146	        pass
147	
148	
149	def _state():
150	    try:
151	        return json.load(open(STATE_FILE, encoding="utf-8"))
152	    except Exception:
153	        return {}
154

156	def _now():
157	    return datetime.now(IST)
158

160	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
161	# GIT helpers
162	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
163	def _git(*args):
164	    try:
165	        return subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, timeout=30).stdout.decode(
166	            "utf-8", "replace")            # git output is utf-8; the Windows locale codec would choke on · / emoji
167	    except Exception:
168	        return ""
169	
170	
171	def _last_commit():
172	    out = _git("log", "-1", "--format=%h\x1f%cd\x1f%s", "--date=format-local:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
173	    if "\x1f" in out:
174	        h, when, subj = out.strip().split("\x1f", 2)
175	        n = len([l for l in _git("show", "--stat", "--format=", h).splitlines() if "|" in l])
176	        return {"hash": h, "when": when, "subject": subj, "files": n}
177	    return {}
178	
179	
180	def _tonight_commits(limit=14):
181	    """Commits since local midnight, newest first, with their scrubbed full diff (bounded so the full site
182	    cycle can never stall on a big night — R-VITALS-STALE follow-up)."""
183	    since = _now().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT00:00:00")
184	    hashes = [h for h in _git("log", "--since=%s" % since, "--format=%h").split() if h][:limit]
185	    out = []
186	    for h in hashes:
187	        meta = _git("show", "-s", "--format=%h\x1f%cd\x1f%s", "--date=format-local:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M", h).strip()
188	        if "\x1f" not in meta:
189	            continue
190	        hh, when, subj = meta.split("\x1f", 2)
191	        files = [l.strip() for l in _git("show", "--stat", "--format=", h).splitlines() if "|" in l]
192	        diff = _git("show", "--format=", h)
193	        out.append({"hash": hh, "when": when, "subject": subj, "files": files, "diff": diff})
194	    return out
195

200	def _transcript_path():
201	    proj = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".claude", "projects",
202	                        "C--Users-[redacted]-Desktop-trading-engines-v2")
203	    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(proj, "*.jsonl")), key=os.path.getmtime, reverse=True)
204	    return files[0] if files else None

207	def _slug():
208	    try:
209	        s = open(SLUG_FILE, encoding="utf-8").read().strip()
210	        if s:
211	            return s
212	    except Exception:
213	        pass
214	    import secrets
215	    s = "transcript-" + secrets.token_urlsafe(18)
216	    try:
217	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(SLUG_FILE), exist_ok=True)
218	        open(SLUG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
219	    except Exception:
220	        pass
221	    return s

223	
224	def _flatten_event(ev):
225	    """One .jsonl event -> a few readable plain-text lines (role, text, tool calls, tool results)."""
226	    role = ev.get("type") or ev.get("role") or ""
227	    msg = ev.get("message") if isinstance(ev.get("message"), dict) else ev
228	    content = msg.get("content") if isinstance(msg, dict) else None
229	    lines = []
230	    if isinstance(content, str):
231	        lines.append("%s: %s" % (role, content))
232	    elif isinstance(content, list):
233	        for b in content:
234	            if not isinstance(b, dict):
235	                continue
236	            bt = b.get("type")
237	            if bt == "text":
238	                lines.append("%s: %s" % (role, b.get("text", "")))
239	            elif bt == "tool_use":
240	                lines.append("tool_use %s %s" % (b.get("name", ""), json.dumps(b.get("input", {}))[:2000]))
241	            elif bt == "tool_result":
242	                c = b.get("content")
243	                c = c if isinstance(c, str) else json.dumps(c)[:4000]
244	                lines.append("tool_result: %s" % c)
245	    return [l for l in lines if l.strip()]
246	
247	
248	def transcript_text(max_events=1500):
249	    p = _transcript_path()
250	    if not p:
251	        return None
252	    out = []
253	    try:
254	        for line in open(p, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read().splitlines()[-max_events:]:
255	            line = line.strip()
256	            if not line:
257	                continue
258	            try:
259	                out += _flatten_event(json.loads(line))
260	            except Exception:
261	                continue
262	    except Exception:
263	        return None
264	    return "\n".join(out)

278	def _page(title, body, refresh=True):
279	    return ("<!doctype html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'>"
280	            "<meta name='viewport' content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'>"
281	            + ("<meta http-equiv='refresh' content='120'>" if refresh else "")
282	            + "<title>%s</title><style>%s</style></head><body><main>%s</main></body></html>"
283	            % (_html.escape(title), _CSS, body))

286	def _journal_body():
287	    st = _state()
288	    c = _last_commit()
289	    line = ("%s | ruling %s | %s | commit %s \"%s\" (%s) | tests %s | files touched %s | next %s" % (
290	        _now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"), st.get("ruling", "—"), st.get("item", "—"),
291	        c.get("hash", "—"), c.get("subject", "—"), c.get("when", "—"),
292	        st.get("tests", "—"), c.get("files", "—"), st.get("next", "—")))
293	    _append_journal(line, c.get("hash"))
294	    tail = []
295	    try:

297	    except Exception:

306	            "No keys, no tokens, no names. <a href='index.html'>build room</a></p>"
307	            "<p class='sub'>Last self-flag: <b>%s</b></p>%s<pre>%s</pre>"
308	            % (_html.escape(flag), eta_html, _html.escape("\n".join(tail))))
309	    return _page("Build journal", body)

312	def _append_journal(line, commit_hash):
313	    """Append a line only when the latest commit changed (keeps it append-only without 2-min spam)."""
314	    try:
315	        prev = open(JOURNAL_LOG, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines()
316	    except Exception:
317	        prev = []
318	    last_hash = None
319	    if prev:
320	        m = re.search(r"commit (\w+)", prev[-1])
321	        last_hash = m.group(1) if m else None
322	    if commit_hash and commit_hash != last_hash:
323	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(JOURNAL_LOG), exist_ok=True)
324	        with open(JOURNAL_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
325	            f.write(scrub(line) + "\n")

328	def _commits_body():
329	    cards = []
330	    for c in _tonight_commits():
331	        files = "".join("<div class='sub h'>%s</div>" % _html.escape(f) for f in c["files"])
332	        cards.append("<div class='c'><div class='h'><b>%s</b> · %s</div><div>%s</div>%s"
333	                     "<pre>%s</pre></div>" % (_html.escape(c["hash"]), _html.escape(c["when"]),
334	                     _html.escape(c["subject"]), files, _html.escape(scrub(c["diff"][:20000]))))  # cap before scrub
335	    body = ("<h1>Commits tonight</h1><p class='sub'>Newest first, full diff, scrubbed of secrets. "
336	            "<a href='index.html'>build room</a></p>" + ("".join(cards) or "<p class='sub'>none yet.</p>"))
337	    return _page("Commits tonight", body)

350	def _files_body(slug):
351	    """R-TWIN-TEST item 5 / MASTER-CHECKLIST item 18: the current repo tree at the transcript's unguessable
352	    path, one file per row, grouped by folder, SCRUBBED — plus a live grep-proof that no token, key or
353	    credential is committed. File contents stay in git; only the tree (paths) is shown. The scrubber redacts the
354	    owner's repo URL, so files are listed, never linked out."""
355	    import subprocess
356	    from collections import defaultdict
357	    # ponytail: git ls-files + git grep run each build cycle; fine at this repo size, cache if it ever slows.
358	    try:
359	        files = [f for f in subprocess.run(["git", "ls-files"], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, text=True,
360	                                            timeout=60).stdout.splitlines() if f.strip()]
361	    except Exception:
362	        files = []
363	    groups = defaultdict(list)
364	    for f in files:
365	        groups[f.split("/", 1)[0] if "/" in f else "(root)"].append(f)
366	    sections = []
367	    for top in sorted(groups):
368	        rows = "".join("<div class='h'>%s</div>" % _html.escape(f) for f in sorted(groups[top]))
369	        sections.append("<details><summary class='h'>%s — %d</summary>%s</details>"
370	                        % (_html.escape(top), len(groups[top]), rows))
371	    # grep-proof: real credential SHAPES (not the word "secret" in code). git grep rc: 1 = no match (clean).
372	    shape = (r'([0-9]{6,12}:[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,})|(ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,})|(gho_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,})|'
373	             r'(sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{20,})|(AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16})')
374	    cmd = ("git grep -InE '(telegram id:secret)|(ghp_…)|(gho_…)|(sk-…)|(AKIA…)'   "
375	           "(matches under tests/ are FAKE tokens planted to prove the scrubber; anything else is a real leak)")
376	    try:
377	        r = subprocess.run(["git", "grep", "-InE", shape], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)
378	        if r.returncode == 1:
379	            proof = "CLEAN — 0 matches for any credential shape across %d tracked files." % len(files)
380	        elif r.returncode == 0:
381	            hits = r.stdout.splitlines()
382	            outside = [h for h in hits if not h.startswith("tests/")]
383	            fixture_files = sorted({h.split(":", 1)[0] for h in hits if h.startswith("tests/")})
384	            if not outside:
385	                proof = ("CLEAN — 0 real credentials across %d tracked files. The only %d shape-matches are FAKE "
386	                         "tokens deliberately planted in test fixtures to prove the scrubber catches them: %s."
387	                         % (len(files), len(hits), ", ".join(fixture_files)))
388	            else:
389	                proof = ("%d match(es) OUTSIDE tests/ — REVIEW as possible real credentials:\n%s"
390	                         % (len(outside), "\n".join(outside[:50])))
391	        else:
392	            proof = "scan could not run (git grep rc=%s); fail closed — treat as UNVERIFIED." % r.returncode
393	    except Exception as e:
394	        proof = "scan could not run (%s); fail closed — treat as UNVERIFIED." % e
395	    inner = ("<p class='sub'>The current repository tree — every tracked file, grouped by folder, scrubbed. "
396	             "File contents stay in git and are never published here. <a href='index.html'>build room</a></p>"
397	             "<p class='sub'>%d tracked files · scanned %s</p>"
398	             "<h2>No secret in the repo — grep-proof</h2>"
399	             "<p class='sub'>Command: <span class='h'>%s</span></p><pre>%s</pre>"
400	             "<h2>Repo tree</h2>%s" % (
401	                 len(files), _now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST"), _html.escape(cmd),
402	                 _html.escape(proof), "".join(sections)))
403	    try:
404	        inner = scrub(inner)                          # fail-closed: if it cannot scrub, do not publish the tree
405	    except Exception:
406	        inner = "<p class='sub'>files page unavailable this cycle (scrub failed, fail-closed).</p>"
407	    return _page("Files", "<h1>Files</h1>" + inner)
408

410	def _index_body(slug, have_transcript):
411	    st = _state()
412	    tlink = ("<li><a href='%s.txt'>Transcript</a> — this session's tool calls and messages, scrubbed</li>" % slug
413	             if have_transcript else "<li class='sub'>Transcript — unavailable this cycle</li>")
414	    tlink += ("<li><a href='%s-files.html'>Files</a> — the current repo tree, scrubbed, + grep-proof of no "
415	              "secret</li>" % slug)
416	    try:
417	        from scripts import vitals                  # R-NOW-LINE §2: the build room shows the same NOW line
418	        now_html = vitals._now_line_html()
419	    except Exception:
420	        now_html = ""
421	    return _page("Build room",
422	        "<h1>Build room</h1>" + now_html + "<p class='sub'>What the desk is building tonight, refreshed every 2 minutes. "
423	        "Everything here is scrubbed of keys, tokens and names.</p>"
424	        "<p>Ruling in progress: <b>%s</b> · %s</p><ul>"
425	        "<li><a href='../status.html'>Status</a> — is the desk up right now</li>"
426	        "<li><a href='journal.html'>Journal</a> — append-only build log</li>"
427	        "%s"
428	        "<li><a href='commits.html'>Commits</a> — tonight's commits with full diffs</li>"
429	        "<li><a href='docs.html'>Docs index</a> — one line per document in docs/</li>"
430	        "<li><a href='supervisor/index.html'>Supervisor</a> — the independent watcher's checks</li>"
431	        "<li><a href='messages.html'>Messages</a> — every line sent + command received</li>"
432	        "<li><a href='reports.html'>Reports</a> — end-of-dispatch + audit reports, newest first</li>"
433	        "<li><a href='tests.html'>Tests</a> — the last recorded test result</li>"
434	        "<li><a href='screenshots.html'>Screenshots</a> — with time, ruling, page</li>"
435	        "<li><a href='queue.html'>Queue</a> — rulings archived / in progress / pending / done</li>"
436	        "<li><a href='eyes.html'>Eyes map</a> — every watcher on the desk</li></ul>"
437	        "<p class='sub'>Last self-flag: %s</p>"
438	        % (_html.escape(st.get("ruling", "—")), _html.escape(st.get("item", "")), tlink,
439	           _html.escape(st.get("flag", "NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD"))))

442	def build(write=True):
443	    """Rebuild the whole /build/ room. Each file is scrubbed before it is written; a file that cannot be
444	    scrubbed is skipped (fail-closed), never published unscrubbed."""
445	    slug = _slug()
446	    txt = transcript_text()
447	    have_transcript = False
448	    pages = {"journal.html": _journal_body(), "commits.html": _commits_body(),
449	             "docs.html": _docs_body(),                    # NB11 item 3: the docs index
450	             slug + "-files.html": _files_body(slug)}      # Files page at the transcript's unguessable path
451	    if txt is not None:
452	        try:
453	            scrubbed = scrub(txt[-500000:])             # cap to last ~500KB before scrub; if this raises, skip
454	            pages[slug + ".txt"] = scrubbed
455	            have_transcript = True
456	        except Exception:
457	            have_transcript = False
458	    pages["index.html"] = _index_body(slug, have_transcript)
459	    if write:
460	        os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
461	        for name, content in pages.items():
462	            with open(os.path.join(OUT, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
463	                f.write(content)
464	        try:
465	            from scripts import mirror                     # R-EYES-COMPLETE: Queue/Reports/Messages/Tests/Shots/Eyes
466	            mirror.build()
467	        except Exception:
468	            pass
469	        sup = os.path.join(OUT, "supervisor", "index.html")   # placeholder until supervisor.py writes its own
470	        if not os.path.exists(sup):
471	            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(sup), exist_ok=True)
472	            with open(sup, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
473	                f.write(_page("Supervisor", "<h1>Supervisor</h1><p class='sub'>The independent watcher runs "
474	                              "every 10 minutes during build hours. Its checks appear here. "
475	                              "<a href='../index.html'>build room</a></p>"))
476	    return {"slug": slug, "have_transcript": have_transcript, "files": sorted(pages)}
477	
478	
479	if __name__ == "__main__":
480	    r = build()
481	    print("build room: %s (transcript=%s)" % (", ".join(r["files"]), r["have_transcript"]))

tool_use Read {"file_path": "[redacted]\\Desktop\\trading-engines-v2\\scripts\\held_log.py"}
tool_result: 1	"""NB19 HELD LOG — held orders as plain "held: <stock> — <reason>" lines for the BUILD ROOM's Messages page
2	(lead-visible, never the public pages — NB9 removed held signals from public).
3	
4	The order path already records each held signal to reports/HELD-SIGNALS.log (TSV:
5	`<ts>\\t[engine]\\t<stock>\\t<pattern>\\tHELD — <reason>`). This module is the plain, de-duplicated build-room
6	VIEW of that log (`plain_lines`) plus an `append` for any caller that wants to write one directly. Pure/light —
7	no heavy import. The build-room page rendering (build_journal) consumes plain_lines(); that render is the
8	launch-path/site wire-in, LIVE via the site build.
9	"""
10	from __future__ import annotations
11	
12	import os
13	
14	_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
15	LOG = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "HELD-SIGNALS.log")
16	
17	
18	def append(stock, reason, path=None):
19	    """Write one plain held line 'held: <stock> — <reason>'. Returns the line."""
20	    path = path or LOG
21	    line = "held: %s — %s" % (stock, reason)
22	    with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
23	        f.write(line + "\n")
24	    return line
25	
26	
27	def _reason(field):
28	    """The plain reason from a HELD-SIGNALS field: the part after 'HELD — ', else the field itself."""
29	    s = field.strip()
30	    return s.split("HELD —", 1)[1].strip() if "HELD —" in s else (s[len("held:"):].strip()
31	                                                                   if s.lower().startswith("held:") else s)
32	
33	
34	def plain_lines(path=None):
35	    """The build-room view: de-duplicated 'held: <stock> — <reason>' lines from the held log, order preserved.
36	    Reads both the TSV order-path rows and any plain lines this module appended."""
37	    path = path or LOG
38	    out, seen = [], set()
39	    try:
40	        for ln in open(path, encoding="utf-8"):
41	            ln = ln.rstrip("\n")
42	            if not ln.strip():
43	                continue
44	            if "\t" in ln:                                   # order-path TSV: ts, [engine], stock, pattern, HELD—reason
45	                parts = ln.split("\t")
46	                if len(parts) >= 5:
47	                    stock, reason = parts[2].strip(), _reason(parts[4])
48	                else:
49	                    continue
50	            elif ln.lower().startswith("held:"):             # a plain line we appended
51	                body = ln[len("held:"):].strip()
52	                stock, _, reason = body.partition("—")
53	                stock, reason = stock.strip(), reason.strip()
54	            else:
55	                continue
56	            key = (stock, reason)
57	            if key not in seen and stock:
58	                seen.add(key)
59	                out.append("held: %s — %s" % (stock, reason))
60	    except OSError:
61	        pass
62	    return out
63	
64	
65	def _demo():
66	    import tempfile
67	    p = os.path.join(tempfile.mkdtemp(), "H.log")
68	    open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(
69	        "2026-09-23T06:06:56+05:30\t[E1]\tAUBANK\tvwap|q4 …\tHELD — moment light not green (spread/depth)\n"
70	        "2026-09-23T06:06:56+05:30\t[E10]\tAUBANK\tvwap|q4 …\tHELD — moment light not green (spread/depth)\n"
71	        "2026-09-23T06:06:57+05:30\t[E1]\tETERNAL\trsi …\tHELD — order gate held (R-HOLD-AND-FIX flag)\n")
72	    lines = plain_lines(p)
73	    # de-duplicated across engines: AUBANK once, ETERNAL once
74	    assert lines == ["held: AUBANK — moment light not green (spread/depth)",
75	                     "held: ETERNAL — order gate held (R-HOLD-AND-FIX flag)"], lines
76	    append("BSE", "spread over the ceiling", path=p)
77	    assert plain_lines(p)[-1] == "held: BSE — spread over the ceiling"
78	    print("held_log demo OK: plain 'held: stock — reason' view, de-duplicated across engines; append works.")

tool_result: 93	def verify(commit, ruling_text="", claimed_step="", brain_fn=None, post_fn=None):
94	    """Run the verifier for one commit. brain_fn(inputs)->{'verdict','evidence'} does the actual exam; default
95	    wires to the active rung. Writes docs/reports/verify-<commit>.md and posts one line. Returns the verdict."""
96	    rung, name, avail = active_rung()
97	    post_fn = post_fn or _post
98	    if not avail:
99	        _write_report(commit, "UNAVAILABLE", rung, name, claimed_step, "no brain rung available")
100	        post_fn("VERIFY UNAVAILABLE %s %s" % (claimed_step, commit))
101	        return "UNAVAILABLE"
102	    inputs = {"commit": commit, "ruling": ruling_text, "step": claimed_step,
103	              "diff": _diff(commit), "template": _read(os.path.join(REPO, "docs", "VERIFIER-TEMPLATE.md")),
104	              "map": _read(os.path.join(REPO, "docs", "VERIFY-MAP.md"))}
105	    brain_fn = brain_fn or (lambda i: _run_rung(rung, i))
106	    try:
107	        res = brain_fn(inputs) or {}
108	    except Exception as e:
109	        res = {"verdict": "FAIL", "evidence": "verifier brain raised: %r" % e}
110	    verdict = str(res.get("verdict", "FAIL")).upper()
111	    if verdict not in ("PASS", "FAIL"):
112	        verdict = "FAIL"
113	    _write_report(commit, verdict, rung, name, claimed_step, res.get("evidence", ""))
114	    post_fn("VERIFY %s %s %s" % (verdict, claimed_step, commit))
115	    return verdict

tool_result: 1	"""NB19 item 59 (OWNER-SAID) — the machine-appended LEAD MISTAKE ledger.
2	
3	One row per lead error: date · what the lead said · why wrong. Appended by the builder when it refuses an uncited
4	name (NEW LAW, CLAUDE.md §24), by the verifier on FAIL, and by the close job when a required order was not placed.
5	The daily close line ends with "Lead mistakes today: N" (count_today). Shown as a page in the build room.
6	Machine-appended, never hand-edited below the header. Times from the machine clock (no typed dates).
7	"""
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	import datetime as _dt
11	import os
12	
13	_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
14	LEDGER = os.path.join(_REPO, "docs", "LEAD-MISTAKES.md")
15	_MARK = "<!-- rows below appended by scripts/lead_mistakes.py — never hand-edit -->"
16	
17	
18	def _today():
19	    return _dt.date.today().isoformat()          # machine clock (R-CLOCK)
20	
21	
22	# NB21: a 🚩 line is READ-ONLY for the owner — it must never ask the owner to DO anything (🙋 is the only
23	# owner-action signal). The guard rejects any 🚩 carrying a sanctioned owner action or a bare imperative.
24	_ACTIONS = ("send this to the lead", "reopen the build session", "double-click", "double click", "log in",
25	            "login", "reply", "tap ", "click ", "delete the", "do:", "you must", "please ", "owner must",
26	            "action:")
27	
28	
29	def is_read_only(line):
30	    """True iff the 🚩 line asks the owner for NOTHING (safe to post). False rejects an action-carrying line."""
31	    low = line.lower()
32	    return not any(a in low for a in _ACTIONS)
33	
34	
35	def flag_line(n, said, why, clock=None):
36	    """The Telegram 🚩 line for mistake #n. Plain words, one line, machine clock (R-CLOCK)."""
37	    clock = clock or _dt.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M")
38	    return "🚩 LEAD MISTAKE #%d (%s) — %s — %s" % (n, clock, said, why)
39	
40	
41	def _count_rows(path):
42	    """How many data rows the ledger holds (date-led rows, not the header/separator)."""
43	    try:
44	        return sum(1 for ln in open(path, encoding="utf-8") if ln.startswith("| ") and ln[2:3].isdigit())
45	    except OSError:
46	        return 0
47	
48	
49	def backfill_lines(path=None):
50	    """Every existing row as a numbered 🚩 line, tagged 'backfill' (NB21 item 3). Read-only-safe lines only."""
51	    path = path or LEDGER
52	    out = []
53	    n = 0
54	    for ln in (open(path, encoding="utf-8") if os.path.exists(path) else []):
55	        if ln.startswith("| ") and ln[2:3].isdigit():
56	            parts = [c.strip().replace("\\|", "|") for c in ln.strip().strip("|").split(" | ")]
57	            if len(parts) >= 3:
58	                n += 1
59	                line = "%s [backfill %s]" % (flag_line(n, parts[1], parts[2], clock="backfill"), parts[0])
60	                out.append(line if is_read_only(line) else
61	                           "🚩 LEAD MISTAKE #%d [backfill %s] — (details in docs/LEAD-MISTAKES.md)" % (n, parts[0]))
62	    return out
63	
64	
65	def append(said, why, date=None, path=None, notify_fn=None):
66	    """Append one row and (NB21) emit ONE 🚩 Telegram line via notify_fn if given — but only if the line is
67	    read-only (asks the owner for nothing). Returns the row text. Escapes pipes so the table never breaks."""
68	    path = path or LEDGER
69	    date = date or _today()
70	    row = "| %s | %s | %s |" % (date, str(said).replace("|", "\\|"), str(why).replace("|", "\\|"))
71	    with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
72	        f.write(row + "\n")
73	    if notify_fn is not None:
74	        n = _count_rows(path)
75	        line = flag_line(n, said, why)
76	        if is_read_only(line):
77	            notify_fn(line)                          # 🚩 posted read-only; an action-carrying 🚩 is never sent
78	    return row
79	
80	
81	def count_today(date=None, path=None):
82	    """How many rows carry today's date — for the close line 'Lead mistakes today: N'."""
83	    path = path or LEDGER
84	    date = date or _today()
85	    try:
86	        return sum(1 for ln in open(path, encoding="utf-8")
87	                   if ln.startswith("| " + date + " |"))
88	    except OSError:
89	        return 0
90

97	    append("used a | pipe", "escaped ok", date="2026-09-23", path=p)

99	    assert count_today("2026-09-23", path=p) == 2, count_today("2026-09-23", path=p)

230	def _run_rung(rung, inputs):
231	    """Production brain. rung (a) = Codex (codex.cmd exec); rung (d) = fresh independent Claude (supervisor
232	    mechanism); (a) falls back to (d) on any Codex failure. Both are handed the pre-run FULL suite result +
233	    its hash and told NOT to re-run the ~21-min suite (it cannot finish in a single shot); they run only the
234	    fast TARGETED checks (module demo + touched-module tests) and confirm the suite hash."""
235	    import sys as _sys
236	    py = _sys.executable                                  # the real interpreter; the 'py' launcher is NOT installed
237	    suite_line, suite_blob = _suite_evidence()
238	    prompt = ("You are the INDEPENDENT VERIFIER. Follow docs/VERIFIER-TEMPLATE.md and run ONLY the TARGETED checks "
239	              "in docs/VERIFY-MAP.md for the touched paths. Run commands with THIS exact Python interpreter (the "
240	              "'py' launcher is NOT installed on this machine): %s\n"
241	              "Targeted checks to run and quote the output of (the -B avoids .pyc writes under a read-only sandbox):\n"
242	              "  1) \"%s\" -B -m engine1_rebuild.conform\n"
243	              "  2) \"%s\" -B -m pytest tests/test_conform.py -q -p no:cacheprovider\n"
244	              "DO NOT re-run the full ~21-min suite: its result was produced ALONE under the RAM gate and is given "
245	              "below as authoritative evidence — confirm the sha256 matches reports/CONFORM-SUITE-RESULT.json on "
246	              "disk. Do NOT modify, stage, or commit any file; run read-only checks only. A claim without a "
247	              "running-evidence line is FAIL. Reply with a first line 'VERDICT: PASS' or 'VERDICT: FAIL' then the "
248	              "evidence.\n\nPRE-RUN SUITE: %s\n%s\n\nRULING:\n%s\n\nCLAIMED STEP: %s\n\nCOMMIT %s DIFF:\n%s\n" %
249	              (py, py, py, suite_line, suite_blob[:1500], inputs["ruling"][:6000], inputs["step"], inputs["commit"],
250	               inputs["diff"]))
251	    out = None
252	    if rung == "a":
253	        cx = _run_codex(prompt)
254	        # Trust a Codex PASS, or a Codex FAIL where the checks ACTUALLY RAN. Fall back to rung (d) only when
255	        # Codex produced nothing OR its sandbox could not execute the checks (owner ruling: rung (d) fallback).
256	        if cx is not None and (re.search(r"(?im)^\s*VERDICT:\s*PASS\b", cx) or not _codex_blocked(cx)):
257	            out = cx
258	    if out is None:
259	        from scripts import supervisor
260	        out, _usage = supervisor._invoke_claude(prompt, timeout=VERIFY_CAP)   # (text, usage) -> unpack
261	    out = out or ""
262	    verdict = "PASS" if re.search(r"(?im)^\s*VERDICT:\s*PASS\b", out) else "FAIL"
263	    evidence = ((suite_line + "\n\n") if suite_line else "") + out            # pin the suite hash into the report
264	    return {"verdict": verdict, "evidence": evidence[:6000]}

tool_result: 1	"""Tonight-only (owner ruling 23 Sep ~01:46): run the full R-EXAM-CONFORM suite under a RAM gate.
2	
3	Waits until 03:00 IST (or until free RAM reaches the 2.0 GB heavy floor, whichever comes first), then runs
4	the suite SINGLE-PROCESS (pytest default; no xdist installed = no parallel workers). Logs free RAM before
5	the run and every 60 s during it. ABORTS the run (kills the pytest tree) if free RAM falls below the sweep's
6	light floor (0.5 GB) or if the 04:45 hard stop is reached; an abort is a FAIL (HELD day). Writes a verdict
7	JSON so the parent session can proceed to the proof only on a genuine green run.
8	"""
9	import json, os, re, subprocess, sys, time
10	from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
11	
12	REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
13	sys.path.insert(0, REPO)
14	from scripts import memory_guard as M
15	
16	IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
17	HEAVY = M.HEAVY_START_GB          # 2.0 GB — the floor RAM should reach by 03:00
18	LIGHT = M.WEEKEND_SWEEP_FLOOR_GB  # 0.5 GB — the sweep's light floor; abort below it
19	RUN_AT = (3, 0)                   # 03:00 IST fallback trigger
20	HARD_STOP = (4, 45)               # 04:45 IST hard stop
21	RAM_LOG = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CONFORM-SUITE-RAM.log")
22	RESULT = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CONFORM-SUITE-RESULT.json")
23	
24	
25	def _ist():
26	    return datetime.now(IST)
27	
28	
29	def _total_gb():
30	    """Total physical RAM in GB (for peak-usage = total - min free). None if unreadable."""
31	    try:
32	        import ctypes
33	
34	        class _MS(ctypes.Structure):
35	            _fields_ = [("dwLength", ctypes.c_ulong), ("dwMemoryLoad", ctypes.c_ulong),
36	                        ("ullTotalPhys", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("ullAvailPhys", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
37	                        ("ullTotalPageFile", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("ullAvailPageFile", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
38	                        ("ullTotalVirtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("ullAvailVirtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
39	                        ("ullAvailExtendedVirtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong)]
40	        ms = _MS(); ms.dwLength = ctypes.sizeof(_MS)
41	        if ctypes.windll.kernel32.GlobalMemoryStatusEx(ctypes.byref(ms)):
42	            return round(ms.ullTotalPhys / (1024 ** 3), 2)
43	    except Exception:
44	        pass
45	    return None
46	
47	
48	def _hm(now=None):
49	    now = now or _ist()
50	    return (now.hour, now.minute)
51	
52	
53	def _log(msg):
54	    line = "%s | free %s GB | %s" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), M.free_gb(), msg)
55	    try:
56	        with open(RAM_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
57	            fh.write(line + "\n")
58	    except Exception:
59	        pass
60	
61	
62	def _write(result):
63	    result["written"] = _ist().isoformat()
64	    with open(RESULT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
65	        json.dump(result, fh, indent=1)
66	
67	
68	def _wait_for_window():
69	    """Return 'go' when it is time to run, or 'no-window' if we passed the safe start time."""
70	    _log("runner armed; waiting for 03:00 IST or free RAM >= %.1f GB" % HEAVY)
71	    while True:
72	        now = _ist()
73	        f = M.free_gb() or 0.0
74	        if f >= HEAVY:
75	            _log("free RAM reached %.2f GB (>= %.1f) — starting early" % (f, HEAVY))
76	            return "go"
77	        if _hm(now) >= RUN_AT:
78	            _log("03:00 reached, free RAM %.2f GB < %.1f — single-process fallback per ruling" % (f, HEAVY))
79	            return "go"
80	        if _hm(now) >= (4, 20):     # too late to start a ~20-40 min run before the 04:45 hard stop
81	            return "no-window"
82	        time.sleep(60)
83	
84	
85	def _run_suite():
86	    if (M.free_gb() or 0.0) < LIGHT:
87	        _write({"status": "aborted", "why": "free RAM below light floor %.1f GB before start" % LIGHT,
88	                "min_free_gb": M.free_gb(), "held": True})
89	        return
90	    _log("SUITE START (single-process). free RAM printed above; total %s GB." % _total_gb())
91	    out_path = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CONFORM-SUITE-PYTEST.txt")
92	    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONUTF8="1", PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8")
93	    of = open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8")
94	    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "tests", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider"],
95	                            cwd=REPO, stdout=of, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
96	    min_free = M.free_gb() or 0.0
97	    aborted = None
98	    while proc.poll() is None:
99	        time.sleep(60)
100	        f = M.free_gb()
101	        _log("suite running (pid %s)" % proc.pid)
102	        if f is not None:
103	            min_free = min(min_free, f)
104	        if f is not None and f < LIGHT:
105	            aborted = "free RAM %.2f GB < light floor %.1f GB" % (f, LIGHT)
106	        elif _hm() >= HARD_STOP:
107	            aborted = "04:45 hard stop reached mid-run"
108	        if aborted:
109	            _log("ABORT: %s — killing pytest tree pid %s" % (aborted, proc.pid))
110	            subprocess.run(["taskkill", "/PID", str(proc.pid), "/T", "/F"], capture_output=True)
111	            break
112	    of.close()
113	    total = _total_gb()
114	    peak_used = round(total - min_free, 2) if (total is not None) else None
115	    if aborted:
116	        _write({"status": "aborted", "why": aborted, "min_free_gb": min_free,
117	                "total_gb": total, "peak_used_gb": peak_used, "held": True})
118	        _log("ABORTED: min_free=%.2f GB peak_used=%s GB" % (min_free, peak_used))
119	        return
120	    rc = proc.returncode
121	    try:
122	        text = open(out_path, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read()
123	    except Exception:
124	        text = ""
125	    m = re.search(r"(\d+) failed,?\s*(\d+) passed", text)
126	    passed = failed = None
127	    if m:
128	        failed, passed = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))
129	    else:
130	        m2 = re.search(r"(\d+) passed", text)
131	        if m2:
132	            passed, failed = int(m2.group(1)), 0
133	    green = (rc == 0 and (failed == 0))
134	    tail = "\n".join(text.strip().splitlines()[-8:])
135	    try:
136	        from scripts import build_beat
137	        if passed is not None:
138	            build_beat.record_tests(passed, (passed or 0) + (failed or 0))
139	    except Exception:
140	        pass
141	    _write({"status": "green" if green else "red", "returncode": rc, "passed": passed, "failed": failed,
142	            "min_free_gb": min_free, "total_gb": total, "peak_used_gb": peak_used,
143	            "held": (not green), "summary_tail": tail})
144	    _log("SUITE DONE: %s (passed=%s failed=%s rc=%s min_free=%.2f GB peak_used=%s GB) — measured floor input: "
145	         "a clean run needs at least %s GB free to stay above the 0.5 GB abort line"
146	         % ("GREEN" if green else "RED", passed, failed, rc, min_free, peak_used, peak_used))
147	
148	
149	def main():
150	    _write({"status": "waiting", "held": None})
151	    if os.environ.get("CONFORM_RUN_NOW") == "1":
152	        _log("START NOW per owner ruling (Edge/Chrome/[redacted] Voice ended); skipping the 03:00 wait.")
153	        _run_suite()
154	        return
155	    w = _wait_for_window()
156	    if w == "no-window":
157	        _write({"status": "no-window", "why": "passed 04:20 IST without reaching a start; not enough time before 04:45",
158	                "held": True})
159	        _log("NO WINDOW — not started")
160	        return
161	    _run_suite()
162	
163	
164	if __name__ == "__main__":
165	    main()
